Active set uniform over top R constraints can be more stable. We can choose p_k = clip((c_k - M + margin)/...) maybe. Let's design a robust "active multiplicative" update:

Pseudo:
```
def smooth_decent(a, t_end, seed, beta_schedule...):
    a = normalize(a)
    c = conv(a)
    best=...
    eta=0.5
    no_imp=0
    while time < t_end:
       c=conv(a); M=c.max(); S=a.sum()
       # weights: softmax of beta * c / S^2 maybe normalized
       x = beta * c / (S*S)  # values ~ beta/n
       x -= x.max()
       # if beta inf-like, top active? Use uniform over all to avoid underflow; but softmax.
       w = np.exp(x)
       w /= w.sum()
       # compute G = 2 * crosscorr(w, a) using FFT or direct
       G = crosscorr(w,a)  # already includes sum p_k a_{k-i}
       # centered
       g = G - G.mean()
       # scale g by maybe max(abs(g))
       g = g / max(1e-12, np.linalg.norm(g)/np.sqrt(n))? for step
       # try steps
       best_eta=eta; best_a=a; best_sc=score
       for mult in [1,2,0.5,4,0.25,8]:
          s = eta*mult
          cand = a*np.exp(-s*g)
          cand = clip(cand,0,1000); normalize
          sc = score(cand)
          if sc<best_sc: choose
       if improved: update, eta*=1.05; else eta*=0.7
       # occasionally perturb/restart
```

Optimization functions:

A. `_lp_direction(f, mode, timeout)`:
- Build dense A? We can use `scipy.optimize.linprog`. For n maybe 416, high iterations. We can avoid building full A via scipy sparse? Dense okay. But solving many LPs: matrix building O(n^2). Use A as np.zeros((2n-1,n)). For each j, A[j:j+n,j]=f. This creates convolution matrix. For vector c constraints, b_ub = c (full convolution of f), ensure nonnegative; for scalar M, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M).
- Objective c_obj=-ones. Bounds (0,None). Maximize sum b.
- HiGHS options: time_limit maybe 1.0-2.0. For vector/same, maybe presolve true.
- Return x clipped, sum. Need handle infeasible? Vector c with c(a) and f, b=f feasible; should be feasible? Constraints f*f=c, so yes. Scalar M feasible. So LP has feasible.
- To reduce cost, use `method='highs'`.
- If timeout, status non-zero maybe x? We can use partial? ignore.
- Direction scaling: g sum. If g sum <= f sum? We still maybe blend.

B. `_lp_iterate(f, stop, mode, seed)`:
- f normalized to sum = sqrt(2n)? Actually scale invariant; keep sum maybe current parent sum to avoid cap? Let's normalize to sum=1 for directions, return scale? Score invariant. For output, any scale. Use sum=1 for stability; then final scale if we need not. Evaluator sum can be 1, score same. But if we return sum=1, sum>=0.01 okay, coefficients ~1/n <1. Good. No cap issue. We'll optimize normalized sum=1.
- Initialize f clipped positive? If zeros, add small eps? Nonnegative allowed; but LP direction with zeros maybe. Use f = max(f, eps) if we need log. For LP linear, zeros ok. But sign? clip to 0. If sum small, ones.
- best update.
- Loop until time:
   c = conv(f), M=max.
   g = LP scalar or vector.
   If g None: add small random; continue.
   Normalize g sum to 1.
   If g equals f (dist small): try alternate mode or random.
   Direction update:
      Option 1 linear: h = (1-τ)f + τ g, clip >=0, normalize.
      Option 2 multiplicative: h = f * (g / max(f,eps))**τ. To avoid zeros: `ratio = np.where(f>eps, g/np.maximum(f,eps), np.ones)`, `h=np.exp(np.log(np.maximum(f,eps)) + τ*np.log(ratio))`, normalize. Equivalent.
   Line search over τ candidates: [current, 2,0.5,4,0.25,0.8,...]. Use fixed set. Evaluate score. Accept best.
   Keep best. If no improvement for many, random perturb or switch LP mode.
- We can alternate scalar and vector. Vector may be more effective.
- To avoid cycles, maintain best and after stall do random reweight: f = (1-η)f + η random positive (e.g. raised cosine), normalize.

C. `_coord_polish(a, stop, seed)`:
- Normalize sum=1. Use direct vector c for O(n) updates.
- For each coordinate i, do 1D optimization.

   * Precompute current c (length 2n-1), S.

      x0=a_i; old_sc=score.
      upper = max(50*x0, 5/n, 0.1?) maybe 1000? But local, use 20x0 or 10*mean.
      Candidate xs = unique positive: 0, x0*mult for mult [0,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.85,1,1.2,1.5,2,3,5] if not too large; if x0=0 candidates [1e-6, 0.5,1,2,5]*mean? Also maybe if upper.
      But if true optimum far, we can expand. Then select best candidate and do golden section in interval between neighboring candidates? Maybe over [0, upper] with 40 golden evals; each eval O(n). That's 416*40=16640 O(n) per sweep ~7M, fine. Use golden but if nonunimodal may fail; combine with grid.
      More robust: sample 32 log-spaced points from 0 to upper, evaluate, take best interval between adjacent grid samples (including 0) and refine with 20 golden. We need handle boundaries.
   * Updating c for candidate x: use c_base=c at start of coordinate. For each eval x, compute delta:
       d=x-x0; if -d > x0+1e-12 skip.
       temp = c_base.copy(); temp[i:i+n] += 2*d*a; if 2*i < len(temp): temp[2*i] = c_base[2*i] + 2*x0*d + d*d.
       Snew=S+d; M=temp.max(); sc=2n*M/(Snew*Snew).
     Need allocate temp each eval; can preallocate `tmp = c_base.copy()` in eval; copying 831*32*416 ~11M floats per sweep, okay. We can optimize by computing max via `np.maximum.reduce`? But okay.
   * Accept best if improves. After sweep, if no improvements, break or perturb.
But coordinate descent changing one coordinate with normalization? Since scale invariant, we can leave S changing. But output sum might drift; normalize at end before scoring. ### convolution and score
```
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
def _conv(a, Lnone=None):
    n=len(a)
    if n<256:
       c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
    else:
       L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
       F=rfft(a,L)
       c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
       c[c<0]=0 # due roundoff
    return c
def _score_from_c(c, n, s): return 2*n*c.max()/(s*s)
def _score(a): ...
```
For performance, maybe use direct for n<=1023? `np.convolve` O(n^2) for 416 ~174k, FFT ~? rfft 1024 ~fast. Direct may be okay for single/few. In coordinate, use direct update not full conv.

```
def _grad_search(a, stop, beta0=5, beta_end=50, seed):
   a=norm(a); best...
   loga = np.log(a+eps)
   for iter:
      c=conv(a); M=max; S=sum
      # weights
      beta = schedule
      z = beta*c/(S*S)
      z -= z.max()
      w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
      # ensure minimum weight 1e-8?
      w = (1-ε)*w + ε*uniform
      # G via FFT crosscorr
      G = _crosscorr(w, a)
      g = G - G.mean()
      if norm g small break
```

```
def _init_shapes(n, parent, rng):
   out=[]
   if parent exists: resample parent to n via np.interp if n != len(parent); also parent itself if n==len(parent)
   out.append(ones)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for alpha in [0.5,0.8,1.0]: out.append((1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x)).clip(0,None)) # high ends
   for freq,phse in [(1,0),(1,np.pi/2),(2,0),(4,0)]: out.append(np.abs(1+0.5*np.cos(2π f x + phase))+1e-3)
   # gaussian bumps centers [0,1], [0.25,0.75]
   # random lognormal: np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n)) normalize with sigma 0.5,1,1.5
   # smooth random via FFT low-pass: generate white, filter, abs
   # parent + noise
   return [normalize(v) for v if sum>0]
```

### LP iteration details enhanced
```
def _lp_iterate(a, stop, seed, modes=None, max_iters=None):
  n=len(a); a=norm(a)
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
  mode_idx=0; stall=0; t=0.35; eta_log=0.5
  while time < stop-2:
     c=conv(a); M=c.max()
     mode = modes[mode_idx % len(modes)]; mode_idx+=1
     b_ub = c if mode=='vec' else np.full(2n-1,M)
     g = _lp_direction(a, b_ub, timeout)
     if g is None:
        stall +=3; random perturb; continue
     g=norm(g)
     # if g sum score better? maybe compute score g; if score(g)<best_sc, blend?
     # Try directions: linear, log
     for style in ['lin','log']:
       for tau in candidates:
          cand = blend(a,g,tau,style)
          cand=norm(cand)
          sc=score(cand)
          if sc<best_sc: update
     if improved stall=0 else stall++
     if stall>10: random restart: a=0.7*best +0.3*random_shape; stall=0; mode_idx=0
     # check if a == best? 
  return best,best_sc
```
Line search candidates maybe dynamic: [0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0]. If best tau near 1, try >1? For linear blending beyond 1 can have negative; allow up to 1.5 with clip. For log interpolation, tau can >1. Use full set [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0,1.3,1.6]. Evaluate all each LP, 9 scores ~9*FFT. LP solve maybe 1s; total okay. We can first evaluate a few; but 9 okay.

Blend:
```
def _blend(a,g,tau,style):
  if style=='lin': return (1-tau)*a + tau*g
  # log:
  eps=1e-12
  la=np.log(np.maximum(a,eps)); lg=np.log(np.maximum(g,eps))
  lb=(1-tau)*la+tau*lg
  # To avoid underflow, subtract max
  cand=np.exp(lb-lb.max()); return cand
```
Log blend with zeros: if a_i=0,g_i>0 and tau>0, la large negative; could underflow. Use eps. Good.

If g has zero components, log blend may force near zero. Fine.

Using vector LP: b_ub=c. Since c can be 0 at edges if support? Then constraints impossible for b positive those shifts? But b=a feasible, with c_k=0 only if a and reversal disjoint? If some c_k=0, b must not contribute to those shifts. a itself has no contribution? For a positive, c_k may be zero? If a all nonnegative and not all zero, c_k positive for all? If support not contiguous, some sums may have zero counts, but if a_i>0 all? Parent positive maybe c all >0. If c_k extremely small, LP still feasible with b=a exactly because a*a=c. Constraints linear in b; a feasible exactly (numerical). Roundoff may make c slight negative? clip to 1e-15. OK.

LP with vector b_ub tighter; solution g might have much smaller sum. We normalize g. Good.

Could also solve LP minimizing something else? But fine.

```
def _kkt_iterate(a, stop, seed):
  a=norm(a); best...
  beta=2.0
  for iter:
     c=conv(a); S=sum
     z=beta*c/(S*S); z-=z.max(); w=exp(z); w+=1e-6; w/=w.sum()
     # maybe mix uniform
     w = 0.9*w +0.1*unif
     T=w[idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]]
     reg = 1e-8 * (abs(T).max()+1)
     try sol=lstsq(T+reg I, ones)
     sol=sol[0]
     if np.all(sol > -1e-7*max(1,abs(sol).max())):
         sol=np.maximum(sol,0)
         if sol.sum()>0:
             soln=sol/sol.sum()
             for tau in [0.2,0.5,1]: cand=(1-tau)*a+tau*soln; sc=score; accept
     # update beta *=1.2 (or if no improve reduce)
```

### Coordinate polish exact implementation details
Let's write efficient `coord_polish(a, stop, seed, max_time)`.
- Input normalized sum=1.
- Loop sweeps:
   c = _conv(a)
   S=1.
   improved_total=0
   # coordinates order random or sequential? Random might help; sequential deterministic. Use rng permutation.
   for i in order:
       if time exceed: break
       x0=a[i]
       # Build candidate xs:
       m = max(S/n, 1e-8)
       upper = min(1000.0, max(100*x0 if x0>0 else 0, 200*m, 1.0))
       # Maybe if x0 is near 0, upper = max(upper, 10*m)
       xs = np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], np.geomspace(upper*1e-6, upper, 20), [x0])))
       # Also add local multipliers around x0 for resolution: if x0>0: x0*[0.5,0.75,0.9,1.1,1.25,1.5]
       # Evaluate. Need temp allocations. For each x:
       d=x-x0
       if d < -x0 - 1e-12: continue
       temp = c.copy()
       # slice
       temp[i:i+n] += 2.0*d*a
       di=2*i
       if 0 <= di < len(temp): temp[di] = c[di] + 2.0*x0*d + d*d
       Snew=S+d
       if Snew < 1e-12: continue
       sc = 2*n*temp.max()/(Snew*Snew)
       track best_x, best_sc
       # If best_x at upper and upper<1000 maybe extend? Could set upper=1000 and rerun? We can just include 1000? For n416 upper max maybe max(100*x0,200m,1). If x0 small 0.5, upper1; but optimum maybe 5? Score very bad if large; not needed. For high coordinate, upper 100*x0 covers reduce/increase.
       if best_sc < old_sc - 1e-15:
           a[i]=best_x
           # Renormalize to sum 1 to keep stable: if sum >0: a*=1/a.sum(); c=_conv(a)
           # But if we renormalize immediately, the baseline c future coords changed; good.
           a = a / a.sum()
           c = _conv(a); S=1
           improved_total+=1
   if no improved_total: break or perturbation
```
But if we renormalize after coordinate, the coordinate move evaluated included sum change but then normalized; score same, c recomputed. Good. However the candidate path with x changed and sum changed may be equivalent to normalized a with coordinate a_i/(S+d), other a_j/(S+d). This move also scales all other coords down, not just one; okay.

One issue: When evaluating coordinate, we kept other coords at current unnormalized values (sum S). Since we normalize after each coordinate, S=1 always. Snew=1+d. Coordinates after normalization: a_i'=a_i/(1+d) if old sum=1? Wait if old sum=1 and we change a_i from x0 to x, then normalized vector has a_i' = (x? Actually set temp vector y: y_i=x, y_j=a_j for j≠i. Sum=1+d. Normalized y_i=x/(1+d), others a_j/(1+d). This differs from simple coordinate ratio. Our score uses y unnormalized, scale invariant, same. Good.

But when updating c for next coordinate, we recompute after normalization. OK.

Cost: `temp = c.copy()` inside eval. For 20-30 evals, okay. Can optimize by precomputing update contributions, but fine.
```

```
### Quick exact shape scoring helper
```
def _norm(v):
 v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel(); v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0); v=np.clip(v,0,1000); s=v.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None; return v/s
```

### _initial_shapes implementation
Need generate vector shapes efficiently. Use rng.
```
def _gen_shapes(n, parent, rng, count=10):
    res=[]
    if parent is not None:
       if len(parent)==n: res.append(_norm(parent))
       else: if len(parent)>=1:
          xs=np.linspace(0,1,len(parent)); ys=np.linspace(0,1,n); v=np.interp(ys,xs,parent); res.append(_norm(v))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    res.append(_norm(np.ones(n)))
    for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0):
       v=(1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x)).clip(0,None) # high ends
       res.append(_norm(v))
       v=(1+alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)).clip(0,None) # high center+ends?
       res.append(_norm(v))
       v=(1+alpha*np.cos(4*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2π))).clip(0,None)
       res.append(_norm(v))
    # smooth random lognormal
    for sd in (0.5,1.0,1.6):
       v=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sd,n)); res.append(_norm(v))
    # tapered endpoints with random interior: v=base * (0.5+0.5 cos(pi*x))? etc
    # random low-pass: white, fft filter
    for _ in range(3):
       w = rng.normal(0,1,n)
       # simple smoothing by cumulative moving? Use np.convolve with Gaussian kernel
       ker = np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-10,11)/3)**2); ker/=ker.sum()
       v = np.convolve(w, ker, mode='same'); v=np.abs(v)+0.1; res.append(_norm(v))
    # end Gaussian two lobes various widths
    for wfac in (0.15,0.25,0.35,0.5):
       w = n*wfac
       v = np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(n)/w)**2) + np.exp(-0.5*((n-1-np.arange(n))/w)**2)
       res.append(_norm(v))
    # parent plus noise
    if parent is not None:
       p = resample parent n
       for sig in (0.1,0.3):
          v = p + rng.normal(0,sig*np.max(p),n); v=np.clip(v,0,None); res.append(_norm(v))
    # filter duplicates/invalid
    out=[]; seen? no.
    for v in res: if v is not None and len==n and sum>1e-9: out.append(v)
    return out
```
Could be many (30); score each shape O(n^2 or FFT) for n416 ~0.001? 30* few ms =0.1s. Good.

But evaluating all for all lengths maybe more; limit count. We can score top few.
```

```
### _lp_iterate with KKT option

```
def _kkt_move(a, beta, seed):
  n=len(a); c=conv(a); S=sum
  z=beta*c/(S*S); z-=z.max(); w=exp(z); w=[...]; w += 1e-6; w/=sum
  w = 0.7*w +0.3*unif? Maybe parameter.
  T = w[idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]]
  reg = 1e-8*(np.abs(T).max()+1)
  sol, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(T+regI, np.ones(n), rcond=None)
  if sol negative significant: return None
  sol=np.maximum(sol,0); if sum small None
  soln=sol/sol.sum()
  # line search blend
  return soln
```
Then in LP loop, after LP directions, also try `soln` for tau candidates. Could be expensive; call every few iterations.

### Gradient softmax local move
Could be cheap and robust. Implement `_grad_move(a, beta, step_candidates)`:
- compute c, S; weights softmax; crosscorr; normalize gradient; log blend; line search. This is similar to LP but no solver. Could be much faster; run many.
Add to LP iter: occasionally use gradient move instead of LP. Or separate `_grad_iterate` before LP.

Gradient move is O(n log n) and can be repeated many times. Let's implement a short `_grad_optimize(a, stop, seed)`:
```
def _grad_optimize(a, stop, seed):
 a=norm; best...; beta=3.0; eta=0.5; no=0
 while time < stop-2:
   c=conv(a); S=sum
   z=beta*c/(S*S); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w+=1e-8; w/=w.sum()
   G=_crosscorr(w,a)
   g=G-G.mean(); gs=np.linalg.norm(g); if gs<1e-14 break
   g/=gs
   # try casc
   best_cand=a; best_sc=score
   for s in [eta*mult for mult in (1,2,0.5,4,0.25,8)]:
      la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - s*g
      cand=np.exp(la-la.max()); cand=cand/cand.sum()
      sc=score(cand)
      if sc<best_sc: best...
   if improved: update; eta=min(5,eta*1.1); beta=min(200,beta*1.05); no=0
   else: eta*=0.6; beta*=0.9 (min1); no++; if no>15: random perturb/break
 return best
```
But gradient on normalized sum? We update log a and normalize; this projects to sum. The gradient we used (centered G) is for scale-invariant objective? It approximates descent direction. Should work. Step scale: g normalized to norm1, so log changes up to eta. If eta=1, ratios e^~2.7 maybe okay. Could explore step negative? Since we minimize, negative gradient (a_i with high G decreases). `loga -= s*g` where g=(G-mean)/norm. G high => decrease. Good. If G is negative? We centered. Good.

But if gradient points to increase max due nonconvex, line search detects.

Crosscorr function must be correct. Let's test manually with a uniform small: n=3, a=[1,1,1]/3, weights w maybe triangular. Crosscorr r[i]=∑ a_q w_{i+q}. For i=0: (1/3)(w0+w1+w2), i=1:(1/3)(w1+w2+w3), i=2:(w2+w3+w4). If w symmetric, g positive center? Good.

Use `rfft` for crosscorr:
```
def _crosscorr(w, a):
  n=len(a); L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
  A=rfft(a,L); W=rfft(w,L) # w length 2n-1, fit L
  r=irfft(A*np.conj(W), L)
  return r[:n]
```
Check with real w, no imaginary. Need ensure circular no wrap for lags 0..n-1; L>=2n-1 as said. Good.
```

### _coord_polish parameter to limit sweeps
Return best found inside; even if time runs out.
```
def _coord_polish(a, stop, seed, pair_moves=True):
  rng...
  a=norm; best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
  sweeps=0
  while time<stop-2 and sweeps<500:
    sweeps+=1
    did=False
    c=conv(a); S=1? after norm
    order=rng.permutation(n)
    for i in order:
      if time>stop-2: break
      # quick coordinate scalar search
      new_sc,_x = _coord_one(a,c,i,n,stop?)
      if new_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
         a[i]=x; a=a/a.sum(); c=conv(a); S=1
         if new_sc < best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=new_sc
         did=True
    if pair_moves and len(a)>? :
       # few random pair moves per sweep (maybe 20)
       for _ in range(20): ...
    if not did: break? Could perturb and continue a few times.
    if sweeps%5==0 and not did: perturb
  return best, best_sc
```

Wait if j slice covers indices k from j to j+n-1; if 2i falls in that range, it adds 2d2 a_{2i-j}. But full change for coordinate j at k=2i when k≠2j is 2d2 a_{2i-j}. That's correct. ### More rigorous coordinate direct update formula
Let's verify with small example n=3, a=[a0,a1,a2], conv c=[a0^2,2a0a1,a1^2+2a0a2,2a1a2,a2^2].
Coordinate i=1 (x0=a1). q=k-i. q valid 0..2 for k 1..3.
For k=1 (q=0 off): c=2a0a1 -> change 2d a0. slice i=1: tmp[1]=old+2d*a0 good.
k=2 (q=1 diagonal): c=a1^2+2a0a2. slice tmp[2]=old+2d*a1; assign old c2 +2x0 d+d^2 = a1^2+2a0a2+2a1d+d^2, correct (2a1^2? Wait old c2 includes a1^2 +2a0a2; new a1^2+2a1d+d^2 +2a0a2. Assign old +2x0 d+d^2, yes)
k=3 (q=2 off): change 2d a2 good.
Coordinate i=0: q=k for k 0..2.
k=0 diag: old a0^2, slice +2d a0, assign old+2x0d+d^2 good.
k=1 off: old 2a0a1, slice +2d*a1 good.
k=2 off: old 2a0a2, slice +2d*a2 good.
Coordinate i=2: q=k-2 for k2..4? slice [2:5]: q=0..2, k=2 off? c2 includes 2a0a2 q0; k=3 off q1; k=4 diag q2. Good.

If n=1, i=0, q=0 diag. slice add 2d a0, assign old+2x0d+d^2; correct.

When normalizing after coordinate, we recompute c, no issue.

Let's draft code in mind.

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def _as_norm(v):
    if v is None: return None
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).ravel()
    if v.size==0: return None
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    return v/s
```

Parent:
```
def _get_parent():
    try:
       h=np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
       h=np.ones(416)
    if h.size==0: h=np.ones(416)
    h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0); h=np.clip(h,0,1000)
    return h
```
If fallback 416? Could use 416 because target. But if no parent, maybe ones(416). Use 416 not 198.

Conv:
```
def _conv(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   if n <= 160:
       c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
   else:
       L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
       F=rfft(a,L)
       c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   return c
```
Maybe for n=416 use direct? Let's choose threshold 128; FFT faster.

Score:
```
def _score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 0.01: return math.inf
   c=_conv(a)
   if c.size==0: return math.inf
   m=float(c.max())
   if not math.isfinite(m) or m<0: return math.inf
   return float(2*len(a)*m/(s*s))
```

Sym:
```
def _sym_if_better(a):
   if len(a)<2: return a
   b=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
   sb=_score(b); sa=_score(a)
   return b if sb<sa-1e-14 else a
```

Crosscorr:
```
def _crosscorr(w, a):
   n=len(a)
   if n<8: # direct
      w=np.asarray(w); a=np.asarray(a)
      L=len(w)+n-1
      r=np.zeros(n)
      for i in range(n):
         r[i]=np.dot(a, w[i:i+n])
      return r
   L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   A=rfft(a,L); W=rfft(np.pad(w,(0,L-len(w))) if len(w)<L else w[:L], L)? rfft accepts input shorter but uses length L; if w length <L, ok. Just rfft(w, L)
   r=irfft(A*np.conj(W), L)
   return r[:n]
```
`rfft(w, L)` pads w to L. Good. Ensure w finite.

```
def _grad_optimize(a, stop, seed):
  a=_as_norm(a); if None return None,math.inf
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  beta=4.0; eta=0.7; no=0
  while time.monotonic() < stop - 2:
     n=len(a); c=_conv(a); S=float(a.sum()) # should 1
     z=beta*c/(S*S); z-=z.max()
     w=np.exp(z); w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0)
     w += 1e-10; w/=w.sum()
     G=_crosscorr(w,a)
     g=G - G.mean()
     gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
     if gn < 1e-14: break
     g/=gn
     cand_best=a.copy(); cand_sc=best_sc
     for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.1):
        s=eta*mult
        loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - s*g
        loga-=loga.max()
        cand=np.exp(loga); cand_sum=cand.sum()
        if cand_sum<1e-12: continue
        cand/=cand_sum
        sc=_score(cand)
        if sc<cand_sc:
           cand_gap...
     if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
        best=cand_best; best_sc=cand_sc; a=cand_best.copy()
        eta=min(3.0, eta*1.1); beta=min(150.0, beta*1.03); no=0
     else:
        eta=max(0.05, eta*0.65); beta=max(1.0, beta*0.9); no+=1
        if no>20:
           # perturb
           shape = rng.normal(0,1,n); shape=np.abs(np.convolve(shape, np.ones(5)/5, mode='same'))+0.1
           pert=_as_norm(0.7*best +0.3*shape)
           if pert is not None:
              ps=_score(pert)
              if ps < best_sc + 0.1: # allow worse? For global, but keep best
                 a=pert
           no=0
           if no? Need limit? Add global_no? If many bad break
        if no>60: break
  return best,best_sc
```

Coordinate polish:
```
def _coord_polish(a, stop, seed, do_pairs=True):
  a=_as_norm(a); if None return None,inf
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
  n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  sweep=0; no_improve=0
  while time.monotonic() < stop - 2 and sweep < 500:
     sweep+=1
     c=_conv(a)
     S=float(a.sum()) # 1
     improved=False
     order=rng.permutation(n)
     for i in order:
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
        x0=float(a[i])
        # old sc maybe use best_sc? current a score known? Use _score? We can compute current sc = 2*n*c.max()/S^2 current_sc
        cur_sc = 2.0*n*c.max()/(S*S)
        best_x=x0; best_c_sc=cur_sc
        m=1.0/max(n,1)
        upper = min(1000.0, max(500.0*x0 if x0>0 else 0.0, 1000.0*m, 2.0))
        xs = [0.0, x0]
        if upper > 1e-12:
           xs.extend(np.geomspace(upper*1e-8, upper, 14).tolist())
        if x0 > 1e-15:
           for mul in (0.2,0.5,0.75,0.9,0.95,1.05,1.1,1.3,1.6,2.0):
              val=x0*mul
              if val<=upper*1.5: xs.append(val)
        # unique with tolerance maybe use set rounded? Could use np.unique sorted.
        xs=np.unique(np.array(xs, dtype=float))
        for x in xs:
           if x < -1e-12 or x > 1000.0: continue
           d=x-x0
           if d < -x0 - 1e-12: continue
           if abs(d) < 1e-15: continue
           tmp=c.copy()
           tmp[i:i+n] += 2.0*d*a
           di=2*i
           if 0 <= di < len(tmp):
              tmp[di] = c[di] + 2.0*x0*d + d*d
           Snew=S+d
           if Snew < 1e-12: continue
           sc=2.0*n*float(tmp.max())/(Snew*Snew)
           if sc < best_c_sc - 1e-15:
              best_c_sc=sc; best_x=x
        if best_c_sc < cur_sc - 1e-15:
           a[i]=best_x
           snew=a.sum()
           if snew > 1e-12:
              a /= snew
           # recompute? current c old for rest; need update c or recompute at start of each coord? If we update a, c of other coords baseline changed. Instead of recompute each time (FFT O(nlogn) cheap if updates few). To be correct, after any update, recompute c and S.
           c=_conv(a); S=float(a.sum())
           if a.sum()<1? normalize done S=1
           if best_c_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
              best=a.copy(); best_sc=best_c_sc # but best_c_sc unnormalized with Snew, same normalized.
           improved=True
        # reset cur? If no update fine
     if do_pairs:
        # limited pair full score
        for _ in range(20):
           if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
           idx=rng.integers(0,n,2); i=int(idx[0]); j=int(idx[1]); if i==j: continue
           orig=a.copy()
           sc0=2n*c.max()/(S*S) # current maybe
           for mi in (0.0,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5):
             for mj in (0.0,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5):
                if mi==1 and mj==1: continue
                cand=orig.copy()
                cand[i] = a[i]*mi
                cand[j] = a[j]*mj
                ssum=cand.sum()
                if ssum<1e-12: continue
                # scale invariant; no need normalize for score
                sc=2*n*float(_conv(cand).max())/(ssum*ssum)
                if sc<best_sc-1e-15:
                    cand/=ssum; best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc; a=cand
                    c=_conv(a); S=a.sum()
                    improved=True
                    break
             if improved: break
     if not improved:
        no_improve +=1
        if no_improve >= 3:
           # add small random perturbation to escape, but keep best
           shape=_gen_simple(n,rng)
           if shape is not None:
              pert=_as_norm((1-0.15)*a +0.15*shape)
              if pert is not None:
                 a=pert
                 c=_conv(a); S=a.sum()
           no_improve=0
           if sweep>50 and no_improve? actually reset. Could allow only a few: use stall counter.
        if no_improve > 8: break
     else:
        no_improve=0
  return best,best_sc
```

### LP iteration implementation

```
def _lp_iterate(a, stop, seed, modes=('vec','scal'), use_kkt=True):
  a=_as_norm(a); if None return None,inf
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
  n=len(a); rng=...
  t_list=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0,1.3)
  mode_idx=0; stall=0
  it=0
  while time.monotonic() < stop-3:
     it+=1
     c=_conv(a); S=a.sum(); M=c.max()
     if not finite: break
     mode=modes[mode_idx%len(modes)]; mode_idx+=1
     b_ub = c.copy() if mode=='vec' else np.full(2*n-1, M)
     b_ub = np.maximum(b_ub, 1e-15)
     g = _lp_direction(a, b_ub, timeout)
     if g is None:
        stall += 5
        # random direction from shape
        shape=_gen_simple(n,rng); if shape: a=_as_norm(0.8*a+0.2*shape)
        if stall>15: break
        continue
     g=_as_norm(g)
     if g is None: stall += 5; continue
     # Evaluate directions
     cur_sc = _score(a) # or 2nM/S^2
     dir_best=None
     for blend in ('lin','log'):

          cand = _blend(a,g,tau,blend)
          if cand is None: continue
          sc=_score(cand)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
             best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc
             if sc < cur_sc - 1e-15:
                a=cand.copy()
                # continue to try others? can't from new a within same direction? We can just set and continue maybe
       # After direction, if a not improved but best improved from tau>1? We set a to best cand? Better set a to best candidate found if it has sc < old a sc.
```

`_blend` log: To avoid log of zero, use eps = 1e-300. If tau >1, (1-tau) negative in log blend? For log interpolation, tau can >1 allows extrapolation: `(1-tau)*loga + tau*logg` okay. For linear, tau>1 may produce negative; clip. For negative tau? no.
```
def _blend(a,g,tau,style):
  if style=='lin':
     cand = (1-tau)*a + tau*g
     cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); s=cand.sum(); if s<=... None; return cand/s
  else:
     la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); lg=np.log(np.maximum(g,1e-300))
     lb=(1-tau)*la+tau*lg
     lb-=lb.max()
     cand=np.exp(lb)
     s=cand.sum(); if s<=... return None; return cand/s
```

### _kkt_move
```
def _kkt_move(a, beta=4.0, mix=0.3):
  n=len(a); c=_conv(a); S=a.sum()
  z=beta*c/(S*S); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w += 1e-8; w/=w.sum()
  if mix>0:
     w = (1-mix)*w + mix*(1.0/w.size)
  idx=np.arange(n)
  T = w[idx[:,None] + idx[None,:]]
  reg = 1e-8*(np.abs(T).max()+1.0)
  I = np.eye(n, dtype=T.dtype)
  try: sol, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(T + reg*I, np.ones(n), rcond=None)
  except: return None
  sol=sol[0] if sol.ndim>1 else sol
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(sol)): return None
  if (sol < -1e-7*max(1.0, np.abs(sol).max())).any():
      # maybe use positive part if most positive
      pos=np.maximum(sol,0); 
      if pos.sum() > 0.5*abs(sol).sum(): sol=pos
      else: return None
  sol=np.maximum(sol,0); s=sol.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None
  return sol/s
```
In LP, after get sol, evaluate blends with sol instead of g (same). To save, call `_try_direction(a, sol, stop, tau_list)`.

### Shape generator simple
Need `_gen_simple(n,rng)` quick for perturb:
```
def _gen_simple(n, rng):
  kind = rng.integers(0,5)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  if kind==0: v=np.ones(n)
  elif kind==1: v=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x)
  elif kind==2: v=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,n)); v=np.abs(v)
  elif kind==3:
     f=rng.integers(1,5); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); v=np.abs(1+0.5*np.cos(2π*f*x+ph))+0.1
  else:
     v=rng.normal(0,1,n); ker=np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-5,6)/2)**2); v=np.convolve(v,ker/ker.sum(),mode='same'); v=np.abs(v)+0.1
  return _as_norm(v)
```

### Resampling parent for other lengths
```
def _resample(parent, n):
   if parent is None or len(parent)==0: return None
   if len(parent)==n: v=parent
   else:
      xs=np.linspace(0,1,len(parent)); ys=np.linspace(0,1,n); v=np.interp(ys,xs,parent)
   return _as_norm(v)
```

### `_optimize_length` detailed code
```
def _optimize_length(n, parent, stop, seed, initial=None, do_lp=True, do_grad=False):
   if n < 8: n=max(8,n)
   start=time.monotonic(); total=max(1.0, stop-start)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   candidates=[]
   if initial is not None:
       ini=_as_norm(initial); if ini is not None and len==n: candidates.append(ini)
   # if parent length diff, resample
   p_res=_resample(parent,n); if p_res: candidates.append(p_res)
   # generic shapes
   shapes=_gen_initial_shapes(n,parent,rng)
   candidates.extend(shapes)
   # choose best
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for v in candidates:
      if v is None: continue
      v=_sym_if_better(v)
      sc=_score(v)
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=v.copy()
   if best is None:
      best=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=_score(best)
   a=best.copy()
   # coordinate initial
   if total > 15:
      coord_stop = start + min(total*0.35, 120.0)
      a, sc = _coord_polish(a, coord_stop, seed+101, do_pairs=total>60)
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
   # gradient if requested and total>120
   if do_grad and total > 120:
      grad_stop = min(stop-10, start+min(total*0.2, 60.0))
      if time.monotonic() < grad_stop:
          a, sc = _grad_optimize(a, grad_stop, seed+202)
          if sc<best_sc: ...
   # LP if do_lp and total>30: leave 20% for final coord
   if do_lp and total>40:
      lp_stop = stop - min(max(20.0, total*0.2), 120.0)
      if time.monotonic() < lp_stop:
         a, sc = _lp_iterate(a, lp_stop, seed+303, modes=('vec','scal','vec'), use_kkt=total>200)
         if sc<best_sc: ...
   # final coordinate
   if time.monotonic() < stop-3:
      a, sc = _coord_polish(a, stop, seed+404, do_pairs=time.monotonic()<stop-30)
      if sc<best_sc: ...
   return best, best_sc
```
Need `_gen_initial_shapes(n,parent,rng)` uses `_as_norm` and avoids too many.

Define `_gen_initial_shapes` returns list of normalized arrays.
```
def _gen_initial_shapes(n,parent,rng):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # ones, cosine pi (end high), cosine 2pi (center high), etc
    for alpha in (0.0,0.3,0.6,0.9):
       v=np.maximum(1+alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x), 1e-6)
       out.append(_as_norm(v))
       v=np.maximum(1+alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*x), 1e-6)
       out.append(_as_norm(v))
    for alpha in (0.3,0.7):
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
       v=np.abs(1+alpha*np.cos(4*math.pi*x + ph)) + 0.05
       out.append(_as_norm(v))
    # end gaussian
    for wf in (0.12,0.2,0.3,0.45):
       w=max(2,n*wf); idx=np.arange(n)
       v=np.exp(-0.5*(idx/w)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((n-1-idx)/w)**2)
       out.append(_as_norm(v))
    # random normal smooth
    for sd in (0.4,0.9,1.4):
       v=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sd,n))
       out.append(_as_norm(v))
    for _ in range(3):
       v=rng.normal(0,1,n)
       k=np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-5,6)/2)**2); k/=k.sum(); v=np.convolve(v,k,'same')
       v=np.abs(v)+0.05; out.append(_as_norm(v))
    # parent resampled already maybe; add parent+noise for lengths close
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       pr=_resample(parent,n)
       if pr is not None:
          sig=0.1*max(float(pr.max()),1e-6)
          for f in (0.5,1,2):
             v=np.clip(pr + rng.normal(0,f*sig,n),0,None)
             out.append(_as_norm(v))
    # dedup by simple? not needed
    return [v for v in out if v is not None]
```
Maybe too many (4*2+2*? 8+4+3+3+6=24). OK.

```
def _final_candidates(a):
```

### Adjust LP direction scale
```
def _lp_direction(a, mode, timeout):
   n=len(a); scale=math.sqrt(2*n)
   f=a*scale
   c=_conv(f)
   if mode=='vec': b_ub=np.maximum(c,1e-12)
   else: b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, float(c.max()))
   A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j] = f
   try res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=..., method='highs', options=...)
   if status==0: b=res.x; b=b-b.min()? no; b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s>1e-12 return b/s
```

Implementation details:
```
from scipy.optimize import minimize
def _lbfgs_optimize(a, stop, seed, betas=(4,10,25,60)):
   a=_as_norm(a); if None return None,inf
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
   n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
      if time.monotonic() > stop - 10: break
      # start y from best (or perturbed for later)
      y0=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
      if bi>0:
          y0 = y0 + rng.normal(0, 0.02 + 0.02*bi, n)
      def fg(y):
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return inf, np.zeros_like(y)
          a = np.exp(y - y.max())
          s=a.sum()
          if s<1e-12: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
          a/=s
          c=_conv(a)
          z=beta*c
          zm=float(z.max())
          ez=np.exp(z-zm)
          Z=ez.sum()
          val=(zm + math.log(Z))/beta
          p=ez/Z
          G=_crosscorr(p,a)
          Cbar=float(np.dot(a,G))
          grad=2.0*(G - Cbar)
          # Convert grad w.r.t a to w.r.t y: grad_y = grad - a*sum(a*grad)? For softmax, grad_y_i = grad_a_i - sum_j a_j grad_a_j. But grad_a_i as computed? We set grad_a = 2G? Actually G=∑p a_shift = 0.5 ∂c? ∂c_k =2 a_{k-i}, so ∂φ/∂a_i =2G_i. Then grad_y = grad_a_i - ∑ a_j grad_a_j =2G_i - 2∑a_j G_j =2(G-Cbar). Correct. We returned grad=2*(G-Cbar). Good.
          return val, grad
      maxiter = 30 if beta<20 else 50
      try:
          res=minimize(fg, y0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':5})
      except Exception:
          res=None
      if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
          a_c = np.exp(res.x-res.x.max()); a_c/=a_c.sum()
          sc=_score(a_c)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best=a_c.copy(); best_sc=sc
      # also evaluate current if res failed? no
   return best,best_sc
```
Potential: L-BFGS may take time and improve. Good.

### Code for `_lp_direction` timeout choices:
```
def _lp_direction(a, mode, timeout=1.5):
   n=len(a)
   if n > 900: timeout = min(timeout, 0.5)
   scale=math.sqrt(2*n)
   f=a*scale
   c=_conv(f)
   if mode == 'vec':
      b_ub = c*(1+1e-8) + 1e-12
   else:
      M=float(c.max())*(1+1e-8)
      b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M)
   A=np.empty((2*n-1,n))
   A.fill(0.0)
   for j,val? We need assign f to column. Loop:
      A[i:i+n,i] = f
   try:
      res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':timeout})
   except: return None
   if res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None
   b=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float); b=np.where(np.isfinite(b), b,0); b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-10 return None; return b/s
```
For n=1024, A creation 2M zeros and loop 1024 slices, okay. But linprog might copy A; memory 32MB. fine.

### Detailed propose schedule code
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    end_safe=t0+budget-15.0
    parent=_get_parent()
    base = len(parent) if parent is not None and parent.size>0 else 416
    if base < 16: base=416
    main_n = min(1024, max(16, base))
    rng_main=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    box = {'a': None, 'sc': math.inf}
    def consider(a):
       v=_as_norm(a)
       if v is None: return
       if v.size > 1024: v=v[:1024]; v=v/v.sum()
       # sym
       b=0.5*(v+v[::-1]) if v.size>1 else v
       sb=_score(b); sv=_score(v)
       if sb < sv: v=b
       sc=sv
       if sc < box['sc']:
          box['a']=v.copy(); box['sc']=sc
    # initial shapes main
    initial_main=[] 
    # parent itself
    v=_as_norm(parent)
    if v is not None and len(v)==main_n: consider(v)
    # generic shapes
    for sh in _gen_initial_shapes(main_n,parent,rng_main):
       consider(sh)
    # If box empty, ones
    if box['a'] is None:
       box['a']=np.ones(main_n)/main_n; box['sc']=_score(box['a'])

    # phase1 main
    phase1_end = min(end_safe, t0 + budget*0.50)
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 10:
       a0 = box['a'].copy() if box['a'] is not None and len(box['a'])==main_n else None
       a,sc=_optimize_length(main_n, parent, phase1_end, seed+111, initial=a0, do_lp=True, do_lbfgs=True)
       consider(a)
    # phase2 other lengths
    phase2_end = min(end_safe, t0 + budget*0.78)
    extras=[]
    for L in (main_n-64, main_n-48, main_n-32, main_n-16, main_n+16, main_n+32, main_n+64, 320, 384, 400, 416, 432, 448, 480, 512, 576, 640):
       L=int(L)
       if 32 <= L <= 1024 and L != main_n: extras.append(L)
    # unique
    seen=set(); extras_u=[]
    for L in sorted(extras, key=lambda x: (abs(x-main_n), x)):
       if L not in seen:
          seen.add(L); extras_u.append(L)
    # limit number to fit
    max_extra = max(3, int((phase2_end - phase1_end)//45))
    for L in extras_u[:max_extra]:
       if time.monotonic() > phase2_end - 20: break
       # allocate time proportional remaining
       remaining_len = len([x for x in extras_u[max_extra?]])? simpler per = max(15, (phase2_end-time.monotonic())/max(1, remaining))
       stop = min(phase2_end - 5, time.monotonic() + max(18.0, (phase2_end - time.monotonic())/max(1, remaining)))
       init = _resample(box['a'], L) if box['a'] is not None else None
       a,sc=_optimize_length(L, parent, stop, seed+10000+L, initial=init, do_lp=(L<=768), do_lbfgs=(stop-time.monotonic()>60))
       consider(a)
    # phase3 final polish on best
    if box['a'] is not None:
       stop3 = end_safe - 8
       if time.monotonic() < stop3 - 20:
          a=box['a'].copy()
          a,sc=_coord_polish(a, min(st0, end_safe-12), seed+20000, do_pairs=True)
          consider(a)
          if time.monotonic() < end_safe - 25 and len(a)<=768:
             a,sc=_lp_iterate(a, end_safe-15, seed+30000, modes=('vec','scal'), use_kkt=False)
             consider(a)
    # final exact
    return _final_evaluate(box['a'], parent)
```

```
def _resample(v,n):
   a=_as_norm(v)
   if a is None or n<1: return None
   if a.size==1: return np.full(n,a[0]) # sum? then norm
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   xs=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); ys=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(ys,xs,a)
   return _as_norm(b)
```

```
def _final_evaluate(a, parent=None):
   ev=globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
   cands=[]
   if a is not None:
      v=_as_norm(a)
      if v is not None:
         cands.append(v)
         if v.size>1: cands.append(0.5*(v+v[::-1]))
   if parent is not None:
      v=_as_norm(parent); if v: cands.append(v)
   cands.append(np.ones(416)/416)
   best_list=None; best_val=math.inf
   for v in cands:
      v=_as_norm(v)
      if v is None: continue
      L=[float(x) for x in v]
      try:
         val = float(ev(L)) if ev is not None else _score(v)
      except Exception:
         val=math.inf
      if val < best_val:
         best_val=val; best_list=L
   if best_list is None:
       best_list=[1.0]
   return best_list
```

```
def _coord_polish(a, stop, seed=0, do_pairs=True):
    a=_as_norm(a)
    if a is None: return None, math.inf
    n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sweep=0; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2 and sweep < 300:
        sweep += 1
        cur_sc=_score(a)
        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur_sc
        c=_conv(a)
        S=float(a.sum()) # should 1
        improved=False
        order=rng.permutation(n)
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
            x0=float(a[i])
            # current score maybe use 2n*c.max()/S^2 to avoid FFT
            cur_sc_local = 2.0*n*float(c.max())/(S*S)
            m=1.0/max(n,1)
            upper = min(1000.0, max(500.0*x0 if x0>0 else 0.0, 1000.0*m, 2.0))
            xs = [0.0, x0]
            if upper > 1e-12:
                xs.extend(np.geomspace(upper*1e-8, upper, 14).tolist())
            if x0 > 1e-15:
                for mul in (0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9,0.95,1.05,1.1,1.3,1.6,2.0):
                    xs.append(x0*mul)
            xs = np.unique(np.asarray(xs, dtype=float))
            best_x=x0; best_sc_local=cur_sc_local
            for x in xs:
                if x < -1e-12 or x > 1000.0: continue
                d = x - x0
                if d < -x0 - 1e-12: continue
                if abs(d) < 1e-15: continue
                tmp = c.copy()
                tmp[i:i+n] += 2.0*d*a
                di = 2*i
                if 0 <= di < len(tmp):
                    tmp[di] = c[di] + 2.0*x0*d + d*d
                Snew = S + d
                if Snew <= 1e-12: continue
                sc = 2.0*n*float(tmp.max())/(Snew*Snew)
                if sc < best_sc_local - 1e-15:
                    best_sc_local = sc; best_x = x
            if best_sc_local < cur_sc_local - 1e-15:
                a[i] = best_x
                sn = float(a.sum())
                if sn > 1e-12:
                    a /= sn
                c = _conv(a); S = float(a.sum())
                if best_sc_local < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best = a.copy(); best_sc = best_sc_local
                improved = True
        if do_pairs and n > 4 and time.monotonic() < stop - 5:
            # limited pair moves
            for _ in range(12):
                if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
                i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
                if i == j: continue
                base = a.copy()
                found=False
                for mi in (0.0,0.5,0.8,1.2,1.6):
                    for mj in (0.0,0.5,0.8,1.2,1.6):
                        if mi == 1.0 and mj == 1.0: continue
                        cand = base.copy()
                        cand[i] = base[i]*mi
                        cand[j] = base[j]*mj
                        ssum=float(cand.sum())
                        if ssum < 1e-12: continue
                        sc = _score(cand)
                        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                            cand = cand/sum
                            best = cand.copy(); best_sc = sc; a = cand
                            found=True; improved=True; break
                    if found: break
                if found: break
        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                # perturb current a a bit from best+random
                shape = _gen_simple(n, rng)
                if shape is not None:
                    for mix in (0.1,0.25,0.4):
                        cand = _as_norm((1-mix)*best + mix*shape)
                        if cand is not None:
                            sc = _score(cand)
                            if sc < best_sc * 1.05:
                                a = cand
                                break
                stall = 0
            if stall > 12:
                break
        else:
            stall = 0
    return best,best_sc
```
Potential bug: In pair `mi==1.0 and mj==1.0` exact float 1.0 if from tuple yes. Good. But if x0=0, cand no change for zero coords; okay.
`_score(cand)` uses FFT; many pair moves maybe 12*24=288 per sweep; if many sweeps heavy. We only call when do_pairs; in `_optimize_length` maybe do_pairs=False in final if time small. For main coordinate stop 120s, 288 FFT* maybe 10 sweeps=2880 FFT; okay.

```
def _lp_iterate(a, stop, seed=0, modes=('vec','scal'), use_kkt=False):
   a=_as_norm(a); n=len(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
   rng=...
   mode_idx=0; stall=0; hard_stall=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < stop - 3:
      it += 1
      if time.monotonic() > stop - 5: break
      c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max()); S=float(a.sum())
      if not math.isfinite(M): break
      # scale etc
      mode = modes[mode_idx % len(modes)]; mode_idx += 1
      timeout = max(0.2, min(2.0, (stop - time.monotonic())*0.25))
      if n > 800: timeout = min(timeout, 0.8)
      g = _lp_direction(a, mode, timeout)
      directors = []
      if g is not None: directors.append(g)
      if use_kkt and it % 4 == 0 and time.monotonic() < stop - 15:
          beta = (50.0, 200.0, 800.0)[it % 3]
          sol = _kkt_move(a, beta, seed + it)
          if sol is not None: directors.append(sol)
      if not directors:
          stall += 5
          if stall > 20: break
          shape=_gen_simple(n,rng)
          if shape is not None:
             cand=_as_norm(0.85*best + 0.15*shape)
             if cand is not None:
                sc=_score(cand)
                if sc < best_sc*1.05: a=cand
          continue
      a0 = a.copy(); sc0=_score(a0)
      cand_best=None; best_dir_sc=sc0
      styles=('lin','log')
      taus=(0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0,1.3)
      for gdir in directors:
         for style in styles:
            for tau in taus:
               if time.monotonic() > stop - 4: break
               cand=_blend(a0,gdir,tau,style)
               if cand is None: continue
               sc=_score(cand)
               if sc < best_dir_sc - 1e-15:
                  best_dir_sc=sc; cand_best=cand
               if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                  best_sc=sc; best=cand.copy()
      if cand_best is not None and best_dir_sc < sc0 - 1e-15:
         a=cand_best.copy(); stall=0; hard_stall=0
      else:
         stall += 1
         if stall >= 5:
            shape=_gen_simple(n,rng)
            cand=None
            for mix in (0.1,0.2,0.35):
                if shape is None: break
                cand=_as_norm((1-mix)*best + mix*shape)
                if cand is not None and _score(cand) < best_sc*1.05: break
            if cand is not None: a=cand
            mode_idx += 1
            stall=0; hard_stall+=1
            if hard_stall > 6: break
   return best,best_sc
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize


def _as_norm(v):
    if v is None:
        return None
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return None
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return v / s


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        h = np.ones(416, dtype=np.float64)
    if h.size == 0:
        h = np.ones(416, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return h


def _conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 128:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        F = rfft(a, L)
        c = irfft(F * F, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    c = _conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _sym_if_better(a):
    a = _as_norm(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        return a
    b = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    return b if _score(b) < _score(a) - 1e-14 else a


def _resample(v, n):
    a = _as_norm(v)
    if a is None or n < 1:
        return None
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(int(n), float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == int(n):
        return a.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    ys = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    return _as_norm(np.interp(ys, xs, a))


def _crosscorr(w, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 16:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            out[i] = float(np.dot(a, w[i : i + n]))
        return out
    L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = rfft(a, L)
    W = rfft(w, L)
    r = irfft(A * np.conj(W), L)
    return r[:n].astype(np.float64)


def _gen_simple(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    if kind == 0:
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 1:
        v = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(math.pi * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        v = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    elif kind == 3:
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        v = np.abs(1.0 + 0.6 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)) + 0.05
    elif kind == 4:
        idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wf in (0.18, 0.28, 0.40):
            w = max(2.0, n * wf)
            v = np.exp(-0.5 * (idx / w) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((n - 1 - idx) / w) ** 2)
            break
    else:
        v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (np.arange(-6, 7, dtype=np.float64) / 2.2) ** 2)
        ker /= ker.sum()
        v = np.convolve(v, ker, mode="same")
        v = np.abs(v) + 0.05
    return _as_norm(v)


def _gen_initial_shapes(n, parent, rng):
    n = int(n)
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for alpha in (0.15, 0.45, 0.80):
        out.append(_as_norm(np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(math.pi * x), 1e-4)))
        out.append(_as_norm(np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x), 1e-4)))

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.65):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        out.append(_as_norm(np.abs(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * x + ph)) + 0.05))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        out.append(_as_norm(np.abs(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(6.0 * math.pi * x + ph)) + 0.05))

    for wf in (0.12, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45):
        w = max(2.0, n * wf)
        v = np.exp(-0.5 * (idx / w) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((n - 1 - idx) / w) ** 2)
        out.append(_as_norm(v))

    for sd in (0.35, 0.80, 1.30):
        out.append(_as_norm(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sd, n))))

    ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (np.arange(-6, 7, dtype=np.float64) / 2.2) ** 2)
    ker /= ker.sum()
    for _ in range(3):
        v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        v = np.convolve(v, ker, mode="same")
        out.append(_as_norm(np.abs(v) + 0.05))

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pr = _resample(parent, n)
        if pr is not None:
            base_sig = 0.10 * max(float(pr.max()), 1e-8)
            for f in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                v = np.clip(pr + rng.normal(0.0, f * base_sig, n), 0.0, None)
                out.append(_as_norm(v))

    res = []
    seen = set()
    for v in out:
        if v is None or v.size != n:
            continue
        key = bytes(v)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        res.append(v)
    return res


def _blend(a, g, tau, style):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != g.size or a.size == 0:
        return None
    if style == "lin":
        c = (1.0 - tau) * a + tau * g
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(c.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return c / s
    try:
        la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        lg = np.log(np.maximum(g, 1e-300))
        lb = (1.0 - tau) * la + tau * lg
        lb -= float(np.max(lb))
        c = np.exp(lb)
        s = float(c.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            return None
        return c / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _lp_direction(a, mode, timeout=1.5):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n > 900:
        timeout = min(timeout, 0.6)
    scale = math.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, n))
    f = a * scale
    c = _conv(f)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if mode == "vec":
        b_ub = c * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-12
    else:
        M = float(c.max())
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j : j + n, j] = f

    try:
        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={
                "presolve": True,
                "time_limit": max(0.2, float(timeout)),
                "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
        return None
    b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-10:
        return None
    return b / s


def _kkt_move(a, beta=80.0, seed=0, mix=0.25):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    c = _conv(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    c_rel = c / (s * s)
    z = beta * c_rel
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0))
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w += 1e-12
    w /= w.sum()
    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * (1.0 / w.size)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    try:
        T = w[idx[:, None] + idx[None, :]]
        reg = 1e-8 * (float(np.abs(T).max()) + 1.0)
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(T + reg * np.eye(n, dtype=np.float64), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        return None

    if sol is None or sol.size != n:
        return None
    sol = np.asarray(sol, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
        return None
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(sol)))
    if amax <= 0.0:
        return None
    if np.any(sol < -1e-7 * amax):
        pos = np.maximum(sol, 0.0)
        if float(pos.sum()) > 0.4 * float(np.abs(sol).sum()):
            sol = pos
        else:
            return None
    sol = np.maximum(sol, 0.0)
    ss = float(sol.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return sol / ss


def _lbfgs_optimize(a, stop, seed=0, betas=(200.0, 800.0, 3000.0)):
    a = _as_norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, math.inf
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 5.0:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 10.0 and bi > 0:
            break
        y0 = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
        if bi > 0:
            y0 = y0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02 + 0.02 * bi, n)

        def fg(y):
            y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
                return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
            aa = np.exp(y - float(np.max(y)))
            ss = float(aa.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-12:
                return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
            aa = aa / ss
            c = _conv(aa)
            z = beta * c
            zm = float(np.max(z))
            ez = np.exp(z - zm)
            ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
            Z = float(ez.sum())
            if Z <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(Z):
                return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
            val = (zm + math.log(Z)) / beta * (2.0 * n)
            p = ez / Z
            G = _crosscorr(p, aa)
            Cbar = float(np.dot(aa, G))
            grad = (2.0 * n) * 2.0 * (G - Cbar)
            grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
            return float(val), grad

        try:
            maxiter = 30 if beta < 500.0 else 55
            approx_iter_time = 0.08
            remaining = max(1.0, stop - time.monotonic())
            maxiter = int(min(maxiter, max(6, int(remaining / approx_iter_time))))
            res = minimize(
                fg,
                y0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-10, "maxls": 5, "maxcor": 10},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            yr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if np.all(np.isfinite(yr)):
                cand = np.exp(yr - float(np.max(yr)))
                ss = float(cand.sum())
                if ss > 1e-12:
                    cand = cand / ss
                    sc = _score(cand)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 5.0:
            break
    return best, best_sc


def _coord_polish(a, stop, seed=0, do_pairs=True):
    a = _as_norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, math.inf
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sweep = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2.0 and sweep < 400:
        sweep += 1
        cur_sc = _score(a)
        if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a.copy()
            best_sc = cur_sc

        c = _conv(a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 2.0:
                break
            x0 = float(a[i])
            cur_local = 2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / (S * S)
            m = 1.0 / max(1, n)
            upper = min(1000.0, max(500.0 * x0 if x0 > 0.0 else 0.0, 1000.0 * m, 2.0))

            xs = [0.0, x0]
            if upper > 1e-12:
                xs.extend(np.geomspace(max(upper * 1e-9, 1e-12), upper, 14).tolist())
            if x0 > 1e-15:
                for mul in (0.2, 0.4, 0.65, 0.85, 0.95, 1.05, 1.15, 1.35, 1.7, 2.3):
                    xs.append(x0 * mul)
            xs = np.unique(np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64))

            best_x = x0
            best_local = cur_local
            for x in xs:
                if x < -1e-12 or x > 1000.0:
                    continue
                d = float(x) - x0
                if d < -x0 - 1e-12:
                    continue
                if abs(d) < 1e-15:
                    continue
                tmp = c.copy()
                tmp[i : i + n] += 2.0 * d * a
                di = 2 * i
                if 0 <= di < tmp.size:
                    tmp[di] = c[di] + 2.0 * x0 * d + d * d
                Snew = S + d
                if Snew <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                sc = 2.0 * n * float(tmp.max()) / (Snew * Snew)
                if sc < best_local - 1e-15:
                    best_local = sc
                    best_x = x

            if best_local < cur_local - 1e-15:
                a[i] = best_x
                sn = float(a.sum())
                if sn > 1e-12:
                    a = a / sn
                c = _conv(a)
                S = float(a.sum())
                if best_local < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_sc = best_local
                improved = True

        if do_pairs and n > 4 and time.monotonic() < stop - 5.0:
            for _ in range(10):
                if time.monotonic() > stop - 2.0:
                    break
                i = int(rng.integers(n))
                j = int(rng.integers(n))
                if i == j:
                    continue
                base = a.copy()
                found = False
                for mi in (0.0, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.6):
                    for mj in (0.0, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.6):
                        if mi == 1.0 and mj == 1.0:
                            continue
                        cand = base.copy()
                        cand[i] = base[i] * mi
                        cand[j] = base[j] * mj
                        if float(cand.sum()) < 1e-12:
                            continue
                        sc = _score(cand)
                        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                            cand = cand / cand.sum()
                            best = cand.copy()
                            best_sc = sc
                            a = cand
                            found = True
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if found:
                        break
                if found:
                    break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                shape = _gen_simple(n, rng)
                if shape is not None:
                    for mix in (0.08, 0.20, 0.35):
                        cand = _as_norm((1.0 - mix) * best + mix * shape)
                        if cand is not None and _score(cand) < best_sc * 1.05:
                            a = cand
                            break
                stall = 0
            if stall > 12:
                break
        else:
            stall = 0

    return best, best_sc


def _lp_iterate(a, stop, seed=0, modes=("vec", "scal"), use_kkt=True):
    a = _as_norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, math.inf
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    mode_idx = 0
    stall = 0
    hard_stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 4.0:
        it += 1
        c = _conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        mode = modes[mode_idx % len(modes)]
        mode_idx += 1
        timeout = max(0.2, min(2.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())))
        if n > 800:
            timeout = min(timeout, 0.7)

        directors = []
        g = _lp_direction(a, mode, timeout)
        if g is not None:
            directors.append(g)

        if use_kkt and (it % 4 == 0) and time.monotonic() < stop - 15.0 and n <= 720:
            beta = (40.0, 160.0, 600.0)[it % 3]
            sol = _kkt_move(a, beta=beta, seed=seed + it, mix=0.25)
            if sol is not None:
                directors.append(sol)

        if not directors:
            stall += 4
            if stall > 20:
                break
            shape = _gen_simple(n, rng)
            if shape is not None:
                cand = _as_norm(0.85 * best + 0.15 * shape)
                if cand is not None:
                    a = cand
            continue

        a0 = a.copy()
        sc0 = _score(a0)
        best_dir = None
        best_dir_sc = sc0
        styles = ("lin", "log")
        taus = (0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00, 1.30)

        for d in directors:
            for style in styles:
                for tau in taus:
                    if time.monotonic() > stop - 5.0:
                        break
                    cand = _blend(a0, d, tau, style)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    sc = _score(cand)
                    if sc < best_dir_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_dir_sc = sc
                        best_dir = cand
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best = cand.copy()

        if best_dir is not None and best_dir_sc < sc0 - 1e-15:
            a = best_dir.copy()
            stall = 0
            hard_stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 5:
                shape = _gen_simple(n, rng)
                cand = None
                if shape is not None:
                    for mix in (0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                        cand = _as_norm((1.0 - mix) * best + mix * shape)
                        if cand is not None and _score(cand) < best_sc * 1.05:
                            break
                if cand is not None:
                    a = cand
                mode_idx += 1
                stall = 0
                hard_stall += 1
                if hard_stall > 6:
                    break

    return best, best_sc


def _optimize_length(n, parent, stop, seed=0, initial=None, do_lp=True, do_lbfgs=True):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 8:
        n = 8
    if n > 1024:
        n = 1024
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, stop - start)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    candidates = []
    if initial is not None:
        cand = _resample(initial, n)
        if cand is not None:
            candidates.append(cand)

    pr = _resample(parent, n)
    if pr is not None:
        candidates.append(pr)

    candidates.extend(_gen_initial_shapes(n, parent, rng))

    best = None
    best_sc = math.inf
    for v in candidates:
        if v is None:
            continue
        v_sym = _sym_if_better(v)
        sc = _score(v_sym)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best = v_sym

    if best is None:
        best = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = _score(best)

    a = best.copy()

    if total > 20.0:
        cstop = start + min(total * 0.30, 120.0)
        if cstop > start + 3.0:
            a2, sc2 = _coord_polish(a, cstop, seed + 101, do_pairs=total > 70.0)
            if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                a = a2

    if do_lbfgs and total > 80.0:
        lstop = start + min(total * 0.20, 90.0)
        if time.monotonic() < lstop - 5.0:
            a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_optimize(a, lstop, seed + 202, betas=(200.0, 800.0, 3000.0))
            if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                a = a2

    if do_lp and total > 50.0 and len(a) <= 900:
        lp_stop = stop - min(max(15.0, total * 0.18), 90.0)
        if time.monotonic() < lp_stop - 5.0:
            a2, sc2 = _lp_iterate(a, lp_stop, seed + 303, modes=("vec", "scal", "vec"), use_kkt=total > 160.0)
            if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                a = a2

    if time.monotonic() < stop - 5.0:
        a2, sc2 = _coord_polish(a, stop, seed + 404, do_pairs=(total > 100.0))
        if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2

    if best is not None:
        best_sym = _sym_if_better(best)
        if _score(best_sym) < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = best_sym
            best_sc = _score(best)
    return best, best_sc


def _final_evaluate(a, parent=None):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    cands = []
    if a is not None:
        v = _as_norm(a)
        if v is not None:
            cands.append(v)
            if v.size > 1:
                cands.append(0.5 * (v + v[::-1]))
    if parent is not None:
        v = _as_norm(parent)
        if v is not None:
            cands.append(v)
    cands.append(np.full(416, 1.0 / 416, dtype=np.float64))

    best_list = None
    best_val = math.inf
    for v in cands:
        v = _as_norm(v)
        if v is None:
            continue
        lst = [float(x) for x in v]
        try:
            val = float(ev(lst)) if ev is not None else _score(v)
        except Exception:
            val = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_list = lst
    if best_list is None:
        best_list = [1.0]
    return best_list


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    end_safe = t0 + budget - 15.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    base_len = int(parent.size) if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 else 416
    if base_len < 16:
        base_len = 416
    main_n = min(1024, max(16, base_len))

    box = {"a": None, "sc": math.inf}

    def consider(a):
        v = _as_norm(a)
        if v is None:
            return
        if v.size > 1024:
            v = v[:1024]
            s = float(v.sum())
            if s > 0:
                v = v / s
        v_sym = _sym_if_better(v)
        sc = _score(v_sym)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < box["sc"] - 1e-15:
            box["a"] = v_sym.copy()
            box["sc"] = sc

    rng_main = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for sh in _gen_initial_shapes(main_n, parent, rng_main):
        consider(sh)
    vpar = _as_norm(parent)
    if vpar is not None and vpar.size == main_n:
        consider(vpar)
    if box["a"] is None:
        a0 = np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)
        box["a"] = a0.copy()
        box["sc"] = _score(a0)

    phase1_end = min(end_safe, t0 + budget * 0.50)
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 8.0:
        init_a = box["a"].copy() if box["a"] is not None and box["a"].size == main_n else None
        a1, s1 = _optimize_length(
            main_n,
            parent,
            phase1_end,
            seed + 111,
            initial=init_a,
            do_lp=True,
            do_lbfgs=True,
        )
        if a1 is not None:
            if s1 < box["sc"] - 1e-14:
                consider(a1)

    phase2_end = min(end_safe, t0 + budget * 0.78)
    extras_raw = []
    for L in (
        main_n - 64,
        main_n - 48,
        main_n - 32,
        main_n - 16,
        main_n + 16,
        main_n + 32,
        main_n + 64,
        320,
        384,
        400,
        416,
        432,
        448,
        480,
        512,
        576,
        640,
    ):
        L = int(L)
        if 32 <= L <= 1024 and L != main_n:
            extras_raw.append(L)

    seen = set()
    extras = []
    for L in sorted(extras_raw, key=lambda x: (abs(x - main_n), x)):
        if L not in seen:
            seen.add(L)
            extras.append(L)

    max_extra = max(4, int((phase2_end - phase1_end) // 40.0))
    extras = extras[: min(8, max_extra)]

    for idx, L in enumerate(extras):
        remaining_time = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
        remaining_counts = max(1, len(extras) - idx)
        alloc = remaining_time / remaining_counts
        alloc = max(12.0, min(alloc, 90.0))
        stop = min(phase2_end - 5.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        if stop - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
            break
        init_a = box["a"].copy() if box["a"] is not None else parent
        aL, sL = _optimize_length(
            L,
            parent,
            stop,
            seed + 10000 + L,
            initial=init_a,
            do_lp=(L <= 800),
            do_lbfgs=(stop - time.monotonic() > 45.0),
        )
        if aL is not None:
            if sL < box["sc"] - 1e-14:
                consider(aL)

    if box["a"] is not None and time.monotonic() < end_safe - 20.0:
        stop3 = end_safe - 8.0
        a3, s3 = _coord_polish(box["a"].copy(), stop3, seed + 50000, do_pairs=True)
        if a3 is not None and s3 < box["sc"] - 1e-14:
            consider(a3)
            if time.monotonic() < end_safe - 18.0 and box["a"].size <= 768:
                a4, s4 = _lp_iterate(
                    box["a"].copy(),
                    end_safe - 12.0,
                    seed + 80000,
                    modes=("vec", "scal"),
                    use_kkt=False,
                )
                if a4 is not None and s4 < box["sc"] - 1e-14:
                    consider(a4)

    return _final_evaluate(box["a"], parent)
```
